- 2026-09-05 (lane C, idea 209 is-the-book-size-floor-a-corpus-wide-clause) — **Idea 199's book-size floor is CONFIRMED where it was measured and REFUTED as a corpus-wide clause: it is a universe clause, and the reversal boundary is the sub-$2B panel and nothing else.** Two independent readings. **(1) The archive census** (the queue's literal ask): all 497 committed CSVs re-read; 17 are usable (publish a varying book-size `n` AND their own OOS Sharpe), giving 135 cells / 70 after collapsing republished corpora. LARGE (u56/broad) rho(n, OOS Sharpe) = **+0.674, 97 of 101 cells positive (96.0%)**; SMALL = **-0.361, 2 of 34 (5.9%)**. Not one of the 17 scripts disagrees on the sign of the panel split, and idea 199's own -0.482 is reproduced (it appears four times under four script names, hence the de-duplicated column). Coverage is the real limit and is stated: **480 of 497 files fix `n` and cannot speak to this either way.** **(2) A fresh corpus with `n` exogenous** — the archive's `n` is almost always derived from a share `m` of the eligible pool, so 384 books were run with `n` as the swept axis itself (3 panels x 2 cost rungs x 6 ranking keys x up to 11 values of n, top-n equal weight among RULES-v1-eligible names, g=0.75, weekly, t+1). u56 **+0.866** and broad **+0.880** with **24 of 24 arm-cells positive** — a *random* ranking key gives +1.000/+0.964, so this is de-concentration, not selection, as idea 199 said; small **+0.197**, 7 of 12, with **four of six keys negative at 25 bps**. The honest small-panel description is therefore **'the effect is absent and its sign is set by the ranking key and the cost rung'**, which is weaker and less tidy than idea 199's flat -0.48/-0.28. **The confound was tested rather than asserted:** on u56 the top of the ladder IS the whole eligible pool (breadth 40) while n=60 on small is a 39% slice, so matching on share of pool — **half the raw large-cap effect is that confound** (u56 +0.677 -> **+0.291**, broad +0.515 -> **+0.299**; +0.29 is the figure to quote, not idea 199's +0.79) — **but the panel gap survives it** (small -0.003 / -0.070). **Rule 8**, six (panel, cost) cells, picks made on <=2016 only, all six floors reported: `n>=25` returns **+0.1657** paired OOS Sharpe vs the IS-Sharpe argmax (t +3.07, 5W/0L/1T) and reproduces idea 199's direction on a corpus it never saw — but the split is **+0.2252 LARGE against +0.0467 SMALL**, and the unbounded `BIGGEST BOOK` is **+0.2992 large / -0.0060 small** while the ORACLE ceiling shows the small panel does hold +0.1453 of findable OOS Sharpe that the floor simply does not find. The ladder is not monotone (k=15 +0.1033 < k=10 +0.1124) so 'k in the twenties', not 25, is what is pre-registerable. Price: 3.6pp of OOS CAGR (14.05% -> 10.44%). **KEEP paths:** 95 of 384 books pass 4a, 28 pass 4b, 48 clear the OOS-window 4b; **26 of the 28 4b passes are large-cap at 10 bps and 0 of 132 small-panel books pass 4b at any n, any key, either cost rung** — independent confirmation of queue idea 136's premise. One drawdown result cuts the other way and is carried: rho(n, -OOS MaxDD) is **-0.43..-0.73 on all three panels, strongest on the small one**, so a size floor may still be defensible as drawdown control everywhere, just not as a Sharpe instrument outside large caps. **By-product KEEP-candidate, memo'd and explicitly NOT proposed:** the rule-8 `n>=25` pick on broad@10bps is top-25 by 6-month return among eligible names, EQW, g=0.75, weekly — 14.1%/1.061/-18.9%, halves 1.219/0.920, OOS 13.7%/1.013/-18.9%, turnover 13.1x/yr, **passing 4a AND 4b on all five bars**, selected out of sample rather than by inspection; blocked by dying at 25 bps, one panel, a thinner H2 (0.920 vs the standing idea-178 candidate's 0.971) and higher turnover (13.1x vs 10.2x). Recorded so it is not rediscovered as new. Rules unchanged, no rules-version bump. Predictions were not pre-registered for this run and none are claimed. Caveats: the panel split is one boundary observed once with no second small-cap universe to replicate on, so the clause could be about breadth or liquidity rather than market cap (idea 195's market-cap leg is still uncached); the t-statistics are descriptive because cells share panels, scripts and one OOS window; survivorship (idea 54) on all three panels; calendar-day index (idea 38) on u56/broad; t+1 only (idea 126). Deterministic, seed 20260905, no hash()-derived seeds (PROTOCOL 5). Script: `research/backtests/2026-09-05_is-the-book-size-floor-a-corpus-wide-clause_C.py` (464 s, 384 backtests).
- 2026-09-05 (lane B, idea 198 does-a-random-screen-de-concentrate-just-as-well) — **KILL of idea 178's IS-window 4b SCREEN as a selector. No RULES change, no new book, no KEEP-candidate.** Idea 199's base pass was IMPORTED and re-run; the 1003-row corpus matches BOTH committed copies (idea 178's and idea 199's) at **0.000e+00** on all 11 metrics with 0 `n` mismatches, and both published edges reproduce exactly (+0.0287 screen, +0.1297 floor) before any new number was read. **The queue's 'if it is inside' branch fires:** against a null that admits a uniformly random subset of the screen's OWN admitted size in each cell (200 seeded draws, same S0 fallback where the screen admits none), the screen's +0.0287 sits at the **54.5th percentile** (null mean +0.0272, sd 0.0139, z +0.11); under the alternative coefficient convention +0.0172 at the **34.0th**. Restriction per se buys +0.0272 of the published +0.0287 — the screen contributes **+0.0015**. **It also fails on its own claimed mechanism:** idea 178 diagnosed de-concentration, and the screen's mean picked book size is 9.73 against the null's 10.49 = the **35th percentile**, i.e. it de-concentrates LESS than chance; in its largest firing cell (C159/broad@10bps, 35 of 98 admitted) it changes no pick and scores +0.0000 against a null mean of +0.0554, the **4th percentile**. **Idea 199's floor survives the same null** — n>=25 at the **100.0th** (z +4.57) and every k in {10,15,20,25} at or above the 95th, with mean picked n 27.82 = the 100th percentile — so restriction per se is not the mechanism and de-concentration is. **The general lesson, and the one number for it:** a random 3-book subset in every cell, with zero parameters, returns **+0.0756** mean paired OOS Sharpe = **263% of the screen's published edge**, because the IS-Sharpe argmax is a concentration machine (mean picked n falls 27.4 → 8.55 as the pool widens, rho −0.988, while within-cell rho(n, OOS Sharpe) is +0.489 (t +3.67) against rho(n, IS Sharpe) +0.195). A PROTOCOL clause 10 is PROPOSED in the memo, not adopted: any selector that narrows a pool before an in-sample argmax must publish its percentile in a size-matched random null. Rule 8 throughout (picks on <=2016-12-31, 2017-2026 read once): S0 13.53%/0.7515/-25.76%, screen 13.45%/0.7802/-25.01%, screen's null 0.7787, n>=25 10.96%/0.8812/-22.23%, RULES v1 5.04%/0.4849/-21.96%, SPY 15.45%/0.8820/-33.72%; both KEEP paths pool-wide 302/1003 (4a) and 162/1003 (4b). Honest limits: the screen fires in only 4 of 11 cells so its null has four live cells — the reading is *cannot be distinguished from* a same-size random restriction, not *proven equal to* one; and unusually for this record every instrument here BEATS do-nothing (dOOS +0.0172..+0.1297), so the objection is not the usual one. 5 of 5 pre-registered predictions hit. Survivorship (idea 54) applies to all three panels. Script `research/backtests/2026-09-05_does-a-random-screen-de-concentrate-just-as-well_B.py`; `.result.md` and `.memo.md` alongside. **RULES.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.**
- 2026-09-05 (lane C, idea 172 the-20-percent-oracle-ceiling) — **ANSWERED, and the recommendation it proposed is a KILL while a narrower one survives. No RULES change, no new book, no KEEP-candidate.** Idea 166's oracle control found only 60 of 300 books (20.0%) have ANY legal gross point inside the OOS-window 4b; idea 172 asked whether the bar that blocks the other 240 is ALWAYS a SHARPE bar, because if it were, 4b's gross-sensitive bars would already be satisfied and the project should stop pricing gross. **3000 genuine backtests** (idea 78's Test B re-run through the engine: B136, k in {20,40,80}, 50 seeded sub-panels each, CAND-n at n in {5,20}, 10 bps, weekly, t+1, 10-point ladder capped at 1.00 by PROTOCOL rule 2), runtime 1736s. **Exactly two tuned parameters** — the ladder point (10 values) and a signed reachability tolerance tau (9 values) — every grid point printed. **Reproduction 3 of 3 exact before any new number was read:** idea 166's committed ladder reproduced on all 3000 rows to **2.220e-16** with the OOS failing-bar string identical in **100.0%**, idea 172's own premise reproduced (**60 of 300**, 114 of 3000 points), and the failing-bar string rebuilt from the five per-bar relative slacks matching in 100.0%. **(1) The answer is NO — 73.8%, not always.** Of the 240 books outside 4b at every legal gross: SHARPE-ONLY **177 (73.8%)**, MIXED 42 (17.5%), GROSS-ONLY 16 (6.7%), and the class idea 172 does not name and this run separates — MISALIGNED, where every bar is individually reachable but no SINGLE ladder point satisfies all five — **5 (2.1%)**. That 5 is the number the recommendation hangs on: it is the only class a finer or wider ladder could ever rescue. **(2) The finding that outranks the headline is per-bar: the DRAWDOWN CAP is unreachable in ZERO of the 240** (reachable 240/240, median best slack **+0.7174**), so every gross-sensitive exclusion in the corpus is the **CAGR FLOOR** (58 of 240; the 16 GROSS-ONLY books are `U={CAGR}` exactly). The caveat is stated rather than buried: DD's reachability is partly mechanical, its modal ladder argmax being **g=0.20** (the ladder's floor) against CAGR's **g=1.00** — the two gross-sensitive bars are reachable at OPPOSITE ends, which is precisely why idea 184 found 4b admission to be a contiguous interval, and why the ladder's only real job is locating the overlap. **(3) Idea 66/173/176's flat-Sharpe result, re-derived from the exclusion side:** median slack RANGE over the 6.75x gross span is H1 0.0071 / H2 0.0040 / OOS 0.0042 against DD 1.0153 / CAGR 1.0524 — **150-250x** — and an unreachable Sharpe bar needs a median **0.13-0.15** of relaxation to close, so the 177 hopeless books are not near-misses. The signed tolerance sweep prices it both ways: relaxing every bar by 0.05 rescues **18 of 177 (10.2%)** SHARPE-ONLY books and by 0.20 only 59.9%, while TIGHTENING by 0.02 costs 13 of the 60 REACHED books and by 0.10 costs 54 of 60 — the reached set passes at a mean of **1.90 of 10** ladder points, clustered at g=0.70/0.75, the incumbent gross. **(4) Rule 8 (everything chosen <=2016-12-31, 2017-2026 read ONCE, 6 cells) SPLITS the verdict:** S0 do-nothing 0.8529 mean OOS Sharpe, S1 IS-Sharpe argmax 0.9394, S2 IS-reachability screen 0.9394, **S3 price-the-gross 0.9373**. Pricing the gross costs **-0.0021 of OOS Sharpe and 3.15pp of OOS CAGR** — a ninth consecutive reading in which the fitted dial does not beat leaving it alone — **but it buys 5.0pp of OOS MaxDD and turns 4a 4->6, full 4b 1->3 and OOS-window 4b 1->2 of 6 cells**, so the gross ladder is a DRAWDOWN instrument that 4b happens to score, which is idea 163's hypothesis arriving from the gross side. Separately, the IS-reachability screen admits 95 of 300 books (31.7%) and selects the **identical book in 6 of 6 cells** — a fourth reproduction of ideas 132/140/151's 'the IS screen changes zero picks'. Benchmarks over the same window: SPY OOS 15.45%/0.8820/-33.72%, RULES v1 on B136 OOS 5.94%/0.5762/-21.19%. **Both KEEP paths on all 3000 rows: 4a 1974, full-sample 4b 157, OOS-window 4b 114 — and NO book is proposed.** The best full-4b row (k=20 draw=19 n=20 @g=0.90, 11.19%/1.204/-12.58%, OOS 10.04%/1.104/-12.58%) is idea 166's MIDPOINT pick re-derived, a re-grossing of a CAND-20 book (idea 144) that fails the OOS-window CAGR floor 10.04% against the 10.82% required; the one prospective pick clearing every bar on both windows is the top-20 of a SEEDED RANDOM 40-name sub-panel, not an implementable universe. **7 of 8 pre-registered predictions HIT**; P4 missed — H1, not H2, is the most frequently unreachable bar (157 of 240), i.e. these books lose in the OOS window's own first half. **Process note, logged rather than quietly fixed:** the first execution swept tau >= 0 only and so read P6 in the tightening direction rather than the relaxation P6 states; the sweep was made signed and the whole run repeated, leaving reproduction, decomposition and walk-forward numbers unchanged. **Report-only clause proposed in the memo, RULES and PROTOCOL untouched: the gross ladder must never be run to satisfy the DRAWDOWN cap** — publish a book's admissible gross INTERVAL plus the CAGR-floor-binding endpoint from one run (idea 184's addendum) and spend the saved compute on cadence (idea 175). For 177 of the 240 no gross clause of any kind helps. Script `research/backtests/2026-09-05_the-20-percent-oracle-ceiling_C.py`; `.result.md` alongside.
- 2026-09-05 (lane B, idea 200 back-fill-the-swapped-coefficient-audit) — **ANSWERED, and the answer is a CLEAN BILL for the published record: ZERO of idea 168's published verdicts move. Rules unchanged; no new book; no KEEP candidate; nothing in RULES.md, scan.py, bot.py or baseline.py touched.** Idea 178's AST audit found 3 of 61 `margins_at` call sites passing `(0.60, 0.70)` into `(phi, delta)` — a CAGR floor of 60% of SPY's instead of 70% and a drawdown cap of 70% instead of 60%, i.e. **both coefficients loosened**, and priced two of them (idea 165 lines 626/641), finding the defect COST that idea. This run prices the third, idea 168's line 515 OOS read, by rebuilding its 352-book corpus through its own imported `weights_k`/`score_k` (2 panels x 11 exponents x 8 shares x 2 cost rungs, weekly, t+1, gross 0.75). **Reproduction asserted before any new number: 2/2** — idea 168's committed `.grid.csv` matched 352/352 rows at **3.55e-15** across all 11 numeric columns with `pass4a`/`pass4b`/`failing` identical 352/352, and the site's own 16 published `OOS_4b_fail` strings reproduced **16/16 exactly** under the swapped pair (OOS Sharpe gap 1.11e-16), so the line-515 read is reconstructed rather than inferred. **(1) AT THE SITE THE DEFECT IS INERT: of the 16 walk-forward arm-cells line 515 evaluated, the failing-bar SET moves in 5 and the PASS/FAIL VERDICT in 0 (0/16 clear under both orders).** Every arm already failed on H1, H2 or CAGR — bars the swap does not move — and all five bar-set moves merely ADD DD. Idea 168's headline sentence survives verbatim at PROTOCOL's bars: 'IS-chosen k beats k=0 on OOS Sharpe 4/4 (+0.084)' -> **4/4, +0.0839**; 'k=-0.5 loses 0/4 (-0.299)' -> **0/4, -0.2986**; 'but fails the OOS-window 4b bars 4/4' -> **fails 4/4**. **(2) CORPUS-WIDE THE EXPOSURE IS REAL AND ONE-SIGNED: OOS-window 4b passes 115/352 (32.7%) -> 74/352 (21.0%), a -35.7% change, with the verdict moving in 41/352 (11.6%)** (u56@10 49->33, u56@25 31->22, broad@10 29->19, broad@25 **6->0**); the newly-binding bar is DD in 27 and CAGR in 14, H1/H2 in none. **Monotonicity was asserted, not assumed: 0/352 books gain DD margin, 0/352 gain CAGR margin, 0/352 flip FAIL->PASS — the defect is STRICTLY PERMISSIVE**, the OPPOSITE sign to idea 178's finding on idea 165, so where it bites it admits books PROTOCOL rejects. **And it bites hardest exactly where idea 168 already warned**: passes fall 17->6 at k=+1.00 and 17->9 at k=+0.75 against 2->0 at k=-1.00, i.e. it flatters the positive-k half idea 168 itself labelled survivorship-inflated — correcting it strengthens that caveat rather than weakening it. **(3) The two tuned parameters are phi x 5 and delta x 5, all 25 points reported:** the 352-book pass count spans **0..123** over the surface, so the swap's **+41** displacement is a third of the whole dial's reach; on the 16 site cells, however, the surface is **0 for every phi at delta <= 0.70** and 3 only at delta = 0.80 — at those cells the CAGR half of the swap never binds at any phi and the DD half binds only beyond the swap's own magnitude, which is the mechanism behind the 0-of-16. **(4) The full-sample 4b column was never exposed at all** — it goes through `H.margins`, which hard-codes the correct roles — and this was proven by reproduction, not inspection: 4a **129/352**, full-sample 4b **47/352** unchanged; books clearing full-sample 4b AND the corrected OOS-window 4b **46**, and **46 under the defect too (+0 admitted into the joint set)**. Rule 8 (fitted <=2016-12-31, 2017-2026 read once, 4 cells): mean OOS A_ISK 17.92%/**0.9144**/-25.32%, A_ZERO 14.75%/0.8305/-23.16%, A_ISKS 16.10%/0.8453/-23.94%, A_LIVE (the live k=-0.50) 5.34%/0.5319/-17.31%, A_ISK-A_ZERO **+0.0839 at 4/4**; benchmarks RULES v1 OOS u56@10 7.73%/0.7471/-13.83% and broad@10 5.94%/0.5763/-21.19%, SPY OOS 15.45%/0.8820/-33.72%; **no arm clears the OOS-window 4b under either coefficient order.** **(5) The exposure is now CLOSED:** an AST re-sweep today finds **62 call sites across 22 scripts, 59 swept/non-literal and 3 SWAPPED — the same three idea 178 named, with no new exposure**; lines 626/641 were priced there and line 515 here, so every exposed site in the record has been re-read at PROTOCOL's bars and **not one published verdict moves.** Predictions **P1-P6 all HIT** (P4 scored on the 16 site cells as pre-registered; the same clause read on the 352-book corpus is FALSE and is reported unscored). Script `research/backtests/2026-09-05_back-fill-the-swapped-coefficient-audit_B.py`; `.result.md` alongside.
- 2026-09-05 (lane C, idea 202 is-the-inert-cell-a-leaderboard-defect) — **ANSWERED, SPLIT: the defect is REAL and exact, the proposed schema flag is a KILL on REDUNDANCY, and rule 8 gives an eleventh consecutive do-nothing win. No new book; no KEEP candidate; RULES untouched.** Idea 191 reported 16 of 180 real overlay cells as never-firing and 8 of its 28 4b passes as those cells, but never tested what that implies. Corpus REBUILT FROM SOURCE not read: 3 panels (U56/BROAD136/SMALL439) x 3 families x 5 thresholds x 2 depths x 2 cost rungs = 180 real cells, exactly two tuned parameters (threshold, depth), all grid points published. Reproduction asserted before any new number: fast_backtest == engine.backtest (1.4e-17/2.1e-17/2.8e-17), the cost identity at the same precision, CAND-20 weights == idea 78/171 at 0.0e+00 on all three, RULES v1 on U56 @10bps at 6.45305%/0.66418/-13.82780% to every digit, and **all 180 cells matched to idea 191's published grid.csv at max|dSharpe| 2.220e-16 / max|d on-share| 1.110e-16**. **(1) THE IDENTITY, proven:** every one of the 16 never-fire cells has a net return series bit-identical to the untilted control's — **max|r_cell - r_control| = 0.000e+00, max|dSharpe| = 0.000e+00** — so every published number on those rows IS the control's. The behavioural superset was tested and is the SAME set here (0 cells that fire yet change nothing, 0 that never fire yet differ). **(2) THE DAMAGE, exactly explained:** 8 of 28 4b passes are inert (**28.6% of the headline**) against **0 of 11** 4a passes; inert passes = (inert cells) x 1{control passes}, predicted vs observed **exact in 6/6 (panel,cost) groups**. The whole contamination is the U56 control clearing 4b on its own, multiplied by the DDCTL thresholds set above the book's worst drawdown. **(3) A SHARPER DEFECT LOOKED FOR AND NOT FOUND:** cells firing in-sample but never in the OOS window would carry a real full-sample on-share and a control's OOS Sharpe (the bar 4b tests) — **0 of 180**. **(4) THE FLAG IS REDUNDANT:** at the LEADERBOARD's own printed precision (CAGR .1%, Sharpe .2f, MaxDD .1%, H1/H2 .2f) 'prints identically to the control' identifies inertness at **precision 1.000, recall 1.000** — 16 true positives, **0 false positives among 164 live cells**, 0 false negatives. The gap is not a missing column but a missing ROW: idea 191 published aggregates, so a reader has no control row to compare against. **(5) RULE 8:** dropping inert cells before an IS-Sharpe pick changed the pick in **0 of 6** panel-cost cases (dOOS Sharpe +0.0000 everywhere) — structural, since an inert cell can only win an IS contest the control wins. Mean OOS Sharpe **S0 do-nothing 0.7766 vs A1/A2 0.6555 (-0.1211)**, the do-nothing arm winning on all three panels at 10 bps and losing only on U56 at 25 bps; picked books clear **0/18 on 4a and 4/18 on 4b**, all four being the U56 control or its near-copy. **(6) BY-PRODUCT RECORD DEFECTS, reported not swept under:** LEADERBOARD.md is **double-appended** — 3034 of 3380 table rows are verbatim repeats (1517 pairs) — and **29.4% of its distinct rows publish no numeric CAGR/Sharpe/MaxDD at all**, so no detector can be applied to them; with repeats collapsed, 57 of 1314 numeric rows (4.3%, 8 scripts, 27 clusters) share an identical printed tuple with a sibling, 3 of them KEEP-bearing. Also, idea 191's on-share denominator includes the 54 warm-up rebalances that cannot move any reported number (dilutes on-share by up to 5.955pp; no config fires only in warm-up, so the inert set is unchanged). **Proposed for Sunday review (report-only, not applied):** do NOT add an inert column; DO publish the control row beside every overlay grid; and repair the double-append. Script: research/backtests/2026-09-05_is-the-inert-cell-a-leaderboard-defect_C.py
- 2026-09-05 (cloud, idea 191 the-on-share-column) — **ANSWERED, SPLIT three ways: idea 186's untested MECHANISM claim is CONFIRMED and quantified, the schema column AS THE QUEUE PROPOSED IT is a KILL, and the on-share SELECTION GATE is the tenth consecutive selector KILL. Rules unchanged; no new book; no KEEP candidate; a report-only PROTOCOL clause 11c proposed for Sunday review.** Idea 186 flagged, without testing, that a circular rotation cannot move the sample's crises and so should be a WEAKER test the higher an overlay's on-share — but its own evidence was CONFOUNDED (DDCTL fired on 4.6-40.5% of dates and cleared 15/36; BUDGET on 53-93% and cleared 2/36, so on-share and family moved together). The single change here is that each family's THRESHOLD grid is widened from 3 points to 5, chosen to span the widest on-share range the family can reach and not to maximise anything: BUDGET now spans **0.4%-93.7%**, DDCTL **0.0%-62.4%**, SLEEVE 9.1%-28.1%. Corpus: 3 panels (U56, BROAD136, SMALL439 — the 483-name sub-$2B panel less the 44 `max_1d_move >= 1.0` tickers) x 3 families x 5 thr x 2 depths x (1 real + 20 rotations) x 2 cost rungs = **3780 rows, 180 real**, plus an **84-real / 1680-null NOISE arm**; base book fixed at idea 2's top-20 EW composite, gross 0.75, weekly, t+1; exactly two tuned parameters (threshold, depth); both cost rungs derived exactly from one 0 bps run via the engine's own turnover series. **Reproduction asserted before any new number:** `fast_backtest == engine.backtest` on all three panels (1.4e-17 / 2.1e-17 / 2.8e-17), the cost identity at the same precision, CAND-20 weights == idea 78/171's `weights_cand` at **0.0e+00** on all three, RULES v1 on u56 @10bps at **6.45305% / 0.66418 / -13.82780%** to every digit, and the null re-validated — on-share preserved in **3600/3600** rotated rows and the circular switch count in **3600/3600**. **(1) THE MECHANISM, via the control idea 186 did not run:** hold the ACTION fixed (de-gross by k) and make the ON indicator PURE NOISE — an episodic Markov chain with no information, matched to the real overlays' median episode length (5.89 rebalances) and driven to a TARGETED on-share — so any real-vs-rotation difference is timing luck alone. The band widens **7.9x, from 0.0909 at 6% on-share to 0.7149 at 86%** (Spearman **+0.677** over 84 cells), and the clause's **realised size is 4/84 = 4.8%, exactly its nominal 1/21**. All four clears are the same single (panel, target) draw on SMALL439 and are reported as one unlucky realisation rather than a trend; the chain saturates above ~86% realised on-share so the 0.95 target is not a distinct point. **(2) THE COLUMN AS PROPOSED IS A KILL.** Within-family Spearman(on-share, clears) is **-0.340 BUDGET, +0.418 DDCTL, undefined SLEEVE (0 clears)**, and pooled on family-demeaned values **+0.068** — because the band rises with on-share (**+0.656**) and so does the realised effect (**+0.595**), and they cancel. The sign is **not even consistent across families**, so a reader given on-share alone would draw the wrong inference on DDCTL rows. **(3) WHAT SURVIVES: on-share is a DEFLATOR, not a predictor.** Conditioning on the realised effect size separates the channels: within the small/mid/large |dSharpe| terciles, rho(on-share, clears) is **-0.141 / -0.445 / -0.127**, and at large effect size the clear rate falls from **0.67** at low on-share to 0.17/0.35 at mid/high. At a GIVEN effect size a higher on-share makes clearing strictly harder. Proposed **report-only PROTOCOL clause 11c**: any state-dependent instrument publishes its realised ON-SHARE **together with** its |dSharpe| against the same book without it, and where both are given, quotes the MARGIN (|dSharpe| - band) as the single summary. Neither number is interpretable alone. **(4) CENSUS:** **632 of 3359 committed LEADERBOARD data rows (18.8%)** rest on a state-dependent instrument — gate 295, sleeve 142, entry/turnover budget 126, drawdown control or trailing stop 83, breadth 26 — and **exactly 1 publishes an on-share**, so the column is missing from 631 of 632 exposed rows. TEXT MATCH: it bounds exposure, re-prices nothing and claims no row is wrong. On-shares are back-filled for all 15 configurations this run prices (DDCTL D=0.03 -> 45.7%, D=0.10 -> 10.4%, D=0.25 -> 0.0%; SLEEVE ma=50 -> 27.4%, ma=400 -> 9.3%; BUDGET tau=0.05 -> 91.8%, tau=0.50 -> 7.6%). **(5) RULE 8** (overlay point chosen <= 2016-12-31, 2017-2026 read once, 18 cells, pool of 10 points, gate = on-share <= the 19.7% corpus median): S0 do-nothing **0.7766** mean OOS Sharpe; S4 on-share-gated **-0.0148 (t -1.38, 5W/9L)**; S5 clause+low-on-share -0.0224 (t -2.35, abstaining in 12); S1 IS-Sharpe argmax -0.0361; S2 idea 186's clause gate **-0.0864 (t -3.14, 0W/11L)**; ORACLE-OOS ceiling only +0.0431. The on-share gate **beats idea 186's clause gate by +0.0716 and the plain IS argmax by +0.0213 — and still loses to doing nothing.** Tenth consecutive instance (110/132/151/166/171/174/175/186/196/191). Benchmarks over the same window: U56 RULES v1 OOS 7.73%/0.7471/-13.83%, BROAD136 5.94%/0.5762/-21.19%, SMALL439 7.88%/0.6617/-32.37%; SPY OOS 15.45%/0.8820/-33.72%. **(6) BOTH KEEP PATHS:** real **4a 37/180 (20.6%), 4b 28/180 (15.6%)**; rotated nulls **4a 25.0%, 4b 20.8%** — a randomly-timed overlay passes 4b MORE often than the real one, the third reproduction after ideas 181 and 186. 4b by panel: U56 27, BROAD136 1, **SMALL439 0** (thirteenth reproduction of idea 136). Two disqualifications stated rather than counted: **16 of 180 real cells are INERT by construction** (on-share exactly 0.0%; DDCTL at D >= 0.15 never fires) and **8 of the 28 passes are those inert cells**, i.e. the untilted control wearing an overlay label; of the remaining 20, **18 are inside their own null band** and the 2 clearers fire on 0.4% of dates against a band of 0.0043. Read honestly, **20 of 20 non-inert overlay 4b passes are indistinguishable from random timing**, reproducing idea 186's 18 of 18. **(7) A SECOND, INDEPENDENT REASON THE COLUMN IS NEEDED:** the null's turnover fidelity on a suppress-a-trade overlay degrades without bound in on-share — BUDGET-skip's mean |null-real|/real reaches **1782.7%** on the widened grid against idea 186's published 25.4% mean / 213.8% max, while DDCTL is 1.1-2.3%, SLEEVE 0.7-1.3% and BUDGET-half 4.3%. Queued as idea 203. **(8) PREDICTIONS: 3 of 5 hit; P1 and P2 MISSED and are reported as failures** — rho(on-share, clears) is not negative in all three families (DDCTL is +0.418, and that inconsistency became the finding), and the band sequence is not monotone across all seven targets (it is monotone over every step where realised on-share actually rises; the last step's does not). Caveats carried, not buried: current-constituent survivorship on all three panels (idea 54) is inherited identically by real and rotated draws so every CLAUSE reading is unaffected and every LEVEL is not; only J-1 rotations exist and neighbouring offsets are correlated so the nominal 4.8% size is approximate (measured directly here and found at nominal); the noise chain saturates above ~86% realised on-share; the census is a text match; idea 38's calendar-day index and idea 126's t+1-only execution carry over. **RULES.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.** Ideas 201/202/203 queued. Script `research/backtests/2026-09-05_the-on-share-column_cloud.py`; artefacts `.console.txt`, `.grid.csv`, `.clause.csv`, `.noise.csv`, `.walkforward.csv`, `.keep.csv`, `.result.md`.
- 2026-09-05 (cloud, idea 196 does-the-leak-free-selector-edge-survive-a-third-corpus) — **ANSWERED: NO. KILL of the replication. Idea 193's S2LF edge was a two-panel accident; the do-nothing streak is restored to NINE of nine. Rules unchanged; no new KEEP; no memo.** Idea 193's rule 8 found S2LF — the IS-argmax over the three leak-free keys MOM/R3/REBASED — beating do-nothing by **+0.1370 mean OOS Sharpe (t +2.19, 11W/1L)** on 6 cost-collapsed cells drawn from TWO panels (broad136, small483), the first non-zero do-nothing loss in nine runs. This run re-priced the IDENTICAL keys, tilt construction (`comp + dir*m*rankpct(key)`), base book (idea 2's top-20 EW composite, gross 0.75, weekly, t+1) and selector protocol on a THIRD corpus — idea 175's **115 books** (SMALL439 after dropping the 44 `max_1d_move >= 1.0` tickers, U56, ETF36 + 112 seeded sub-draws; NO broad136 anywhere), rebuilt from idea 175's own seeds. **4370 rows / 4140 real arms / 2185 backtests**, exactly two tuned parameters (m in {0.20,0.50,1.00}, dir in {POS,NEG}), every grid point reported; the 25 bps rung derived exactly from one 0 bps run via the engine's own turnover series. **Five reproduction checks asserted before any new number was read:** RULES v1 on u56 @10bps at **6.45305% / 0.66418 / -13.82780%** to every published digit, `fast_backtest == engine.backtest` at **1.39e-17**, the cost identity at **1.39e-17**, idea 175's published U56 @ W ladder point rebuilt at **12.8581% / 1.1075 / -18.2071%** (published 12.86/1.1075/-18.21), and the corpus at **115/115 books** (SMALL 49 / U56 33 / ETF 33). **(1) THE HEADLINE: it does not replicate, it inverts.** Rule 8 over 690 cells (key/dir chosen <= 2016-12-31, 2017-2026 read once): S0 do-nothing **0.6312** mean OOS Sharpe; **S2LF -0.0032 (t -1.79, 172W/228L)**; on idea 193's own cost-collapsed convention (345 cells) **-0.0032, t -1.28, 84W/116L** against its published +0.1370 / t +2.19 / 11W-1L. The point estimate moves 0.140 of OOS Sharpe and changes sign on 58x the paired observations. ORACLE-OOS, the unreachable ceiling, is only **+0.0210** — the whole reachable set on this corpus is one seventh the size of the effect idea 193 reported. **(2) THE NEW THING, and it is not a null result:** the selector is genuinely SKILLED — it agrees with the OOS oracle in **52.9%** of cells against 16.7% by chance and **beats RANDOM drawn from its own pool by +0.0110 (t +3.78, 193W/147L)** — but the POOL has negative expectancy: mean dSharpe over all 4140 real arms is negative and monotone in tilt strength (**-0.0011 at m=0.2 -> -0.0150 at m=1.0 @10bps; -0.0014 -> -0.0225 @25bps**). **Real picking skill inside a negative-expectancy pool still loses to not trading the instrument at all**, which is the distinction idea 193's 6 cells could not draw and which reconciles this run with idea 189: the best CONSTANT (C-MOM/POS **+0.0049, t +3.64**) is the ONLY arm in the run that beats S0. Queued as ideas 204/205. **(3) NO FAMILY CARRIES IT.** SMALL (49 books) **-0.0004 (t -0.11)**, U56 (33) -0.0053 (t -2.35), ETF (33) -0.0051 (t -4.31). The SMALL family here is sub-draws of idea 193's OWN small483 panel and reads exactly zero, so the parent's effect was the broad136 leg or the 6-cell sample, not the keys. **(4) BOTH KEEP PATHS on all 4140 real arms: 4a 1665, 4b 208** (controls 94/230 and 14/230); **4b is U56 208 / SMALL 0 / ETF 0** — the twelfth reproduction of idea 136 and the first run to show ETF36's family has no 4b-passing book either. Among 3932 failing rows the bar-violation counts are **CAGR 3893 / OOS-Sharpe 2632 / DD 1211**. Benchmarks: U56 RULES v1 6.45%/0.6642/-13.83% (OOS 7.73%/0.7471), SMALL439 7.41%/0.5647/-36.12% (OOS 6.35%/0.4923), ETF36 3.80%/0.4651/-17.87% (OOS 3.78%/0.4425); SPY 15.23%/0.8890/-33.72% (OOS 15.45%/0.8820). **(5) BY-PRODUCT, PARKed and explicitly NOT proposed:** `U56 + REBASED/POS` clears 4b at all three m and both cost rungs (m=1.0 @10bps **13.30% / 1.1327 / -18.44%**, halves 1.189/1.107, **OOS 1.2007 / 15.60% CAGR**, 7.9x/yr) and is the only arm in the run that improves the standing 4b candidate on Sharpe, CAGR, drawdown AND turnover at once — but it **fails the universe change outright** (SMALL439 0.2408, ETF36 0.8657 against SPY 0.8615/0.8890; 0 of 1188 ETF and 0 of 1764 SMALL rows pass 4b), fails 4a in all six cells, and per idea 144 is idea 2's existing book with an instrument on it. Idea 206 prices it against its own gross ladder first. **(6) PREDICTIONS: 3 of 5 hit; P1 and P2 MISSED and are reported as failures** — S2LF is not positive-but-smaller (it is negative), and it DOES significantly beat RANDOM, which became the run's real content. Caveats carried, not buried: current-constituent survivorship on all three families (idea 54) is inherited identically by control and arms so the PAIRED reading is unaffected and every LEVEL is not; books inside a family are overlapping draws from one pool so the pooled t OVERSTATES significance (per-family numbers given for that reason, and the conclusion does not rest on the pooled t); idea 38's calendar-day index on U56/ETF36 after 2014-09-17; idea 126's t+1-only execution; REBASED's 'entry' is the first bar of the panel sample, not a listing date (idea 193's construction, re-priced rather than improved). **RULES.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.** Ideas 204/205/206 queued. Script `research/backtests/2026-09-05_does-the-leak-free-selector-edge-survive-a-third-corpus_cloud.py`; artefacts `.console.txt`, `.arms.csv`, `.walkforward.csv`, `.keep.csv`, `.result.md`.

Benchmarks: U56 RULES v1 6.45%/0.6642/-13.83% (OOS 7.73%/0.7471), SMALL439 7.41%/0.5647/-36.12% (OOS 6.35%/0.4923), ETF36 3.80%/0.4651/-17.87% (OOS 3.78%/0.4425); SPY 15.23%/0.8890/-33.72% (OOS 15.45%/0.8820). **(5) BY-PRODUCT, PARKed and explicitly NOT proposed:** `U56 + REBASED/POS` clears 4b at all three m and both cost rungs (m=1.0 @10bps **13.30% / 1.1327 / -18.44%**, halves 1.189/1.107, **OOS 1.2007 / 15.60% CAGR**, 7.9x/yr) and is the only arm in the run that improves the standing 4b candidate on Sharpe, CAGR, drawdown AND turnover at once — but it **fails the universe change outright** (SMALL439 0.2408, ETF36 0.8657 against SPY 0.8615/0.8890; 0 of 1188 ETF and 0 of 1764 SMALL rows pass 4b), fails 4a in all six cells, and per idea 144 is idea 2's existing book with an instrument on it. Idea 200 prices it against its own gross ladder first. **(6) PREDICTIONS: 3 of 5 hit; P1 and P2 MISSED and are reported as failures** — S2LF is not positive-but-smaller (it is negative), and it DOES significantly beat RANDOM, which became the run's real content. Caveats carried, not buried: current-constituent survivorship on all three families (idea 54) is inherited identically by control and arms so the PAIRED reading is unaffected and every LEVEL is not; books inside a family are overlapping draws from one pool so the pooled t OVERSTATES significance (per-family numbers given for that reason, and the conclusion does not rest on the pooled t); idea 38's calendar-day index on U56/ETF36 after 2014-09-17; idea 126's t+1-only execution; REBASED's 'entry' is the first bar of the panel sample, not a listing date (idea 193's construction, re-priced rather than improved). **RULES.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.** Ideas 198/199/200 queued. Script `research/backtests/2026-09-05_does-the-leak-free-selector-edge-survive-a-third-corpus_cloud.py`; artefacts `.console.txt`, `.arms.csv`, `.walkforward.csv`, `.keep.csv`, `.result.md`.
- 2026-09-05 (lane B, idea 193 is-PRICE/NEG-the-only-arm-that-is-both-large-and-helpful) — **ANSWERED, NO, and it invalidates a KEY rather than an arm: KILL of every price-LEVEL key in the record. No RULES change, no new book, no KEEP-candidate.** Idea 192 found 17 positive clearers among 288 arms, 12 of them PRICE/NEG, and asked whether any survives when price is replaced by frozen-at-entry rank, dollar volume or market cap. It does not — and the reason is an identity, not a data accident. `load_universe()` serves auto-adjusted closes, so the series IS a total-return index and **`px[T]/px[t]` IS the cumulative total return from t to T** (verified to **3.18e-12** on broad, 4.26e-13 on small). Therefore `log PRICE_t = log px[T] − log TR(t→T)`: the cross-sectional rank of a price LEVEL is terminal price minus **realised future total return**, neither term knowable at t. **204 real arms + 240 matched-null arms + 4 controls = 448 backtests**, 2 panels (broad and small carry all 17 clearers; none is on u56), idea 2's base book (top-20 EW, 75% gross, weekly, t+1), both cost rungs derived from one 0 bps run via the engine's own identity. **Exactly two tuned parameters** (m over 3 values, direction over 2), both inherited from idea 181 and printed in full; the KEY is a reported axis. **Reproduction 6 of 6 before any new number was read:** RULES v1 on u56 at **6.45305% / 0.66418 / −13.82780%** to every printed digit, `fast_backtest == engine.backtest` at **2.08e-17**, the cost identity at **2.08e-17**, idea 181's **72 published (panel,key,dir,m,cost) cells rebuilt from scratch at max |dSharpe − published| = 4.16e-16**, its **24 matched-null bands from the parent's own seed at 3.19e-16**, and **0 clause verdict mismatches**. **(1) THE ATTRIBUTION.** Running the two halves of the identity as their own arms: **FWDRET/POS (the future-return term alone) delivers +0.6921 mean dSharpe against PRICE/NEG's +0.3532 — share +1.879 — while PXTERM/NEG (the terminal-level term alone) delivers −0.1514, share −0.373.** The published effect is entirely the look-ahead term and the level term works against it; mean cross-sectional Spearman(PRICE, FWDRET) is −0.296 (broad) / −0.424 (small). **(2) THE THREE SUBSTITUTIONS.** FROZEN (the queue's own proposed fix) does **not** replicate — +0.1621 broad / +0.1633 small, clearing-and-positive 6/12 on broad but **0/12 on small**, 4b 0/24. **MCAP is PARKED with 'needs local/Actions data'**: no shares-outstanding series is cached and the sandbox has no internet. DVOL (20d mean dollar volume, small panel only) initially looked like the first genuine causal find of the project — **+0.5777 mean dSharpe, larger than PRICE/NEG on the same panel (+0.4663), clearing-and-positive at 6 of 6 NEG points, 4a 3/12, and 6/6 wins in its rule-8 cells.** **(3) IT IS THE SAME LEAK** (a post-hoc control, declared as such wherever it appears): DVOL multiplies the ADJUSTED close, so it carries the same factor. Against FWDRET the Spearmans are **DVOL −0.596, VOLSH (share volume only) −0.417, DVOLT (terminal price × volume, factor removed) −0.096**, and the effect sizes track that monotonically — **+0.578 / +0.323 / +0.142** with clearing-and-positive counts **6/12 / 5/12 / 0/12** — while DVOL and DVOLT rank-correlate at **+0.817**, i.e. they measure nearly the same liquidity cross-section and only the leaky one works. **(4) THE ANSWER.** Of 204 real arms, 50 are clearing-and-positive; 43 are a price-level key, a volume key carrying a price, or an outright oracle. The **7 leak-free** ones (MOM/NEG ×1, R3/NEG ×4, REBASED/NEG ×2) are all at the smallest tilt m = 0.20, clear their band by **0.0091–0.0375**, and **none passes 4b**. Both KEEP paths over the whole grid: **4a 62/204, 4b 4/204**, and the only clearing-and-positive arm that passes 4b anywhere in the run is the ORACLE FWDRET/POS (broad, m=1.0 @10bps: 23.67% / 1.5414 / −20.21%, OOS Sharpe 1.7411). **(5) RULE 8** (key chosen ≤2016-12-31, 2017-2026 read once, 12 cells = 2 panels × 3 m × 2 rungs, oracle keys excluded from every selector): **the eight-run streak of do-nothing wins ENDS, but every large win is carried by a key that knows the future.** S0 do-nothing 0.6061 mean OOS Sharpe; C-DVOL/NEG **+0.8088** (6 cells), C-VOLSH/NEG +0.5042 (6), C-PRICE/NEG +0.4500 (12/12 wins), S1 IS-argmax +0.4030, S2 causal-as-pre-registered +0.3720 — against leak-free **S2LF +0.1370 (t +2.19 over 6 cost-collapsed cells, 11W/1L)**; S3 clause+positive +0.0137, abstaining in 10 of 12. Benchmarks on the same window: broad RULES v1 5.94%/0.5763/−21.19%, small RULES v1 6.35%/0.4923/−36.12%, SPY 15.45%/0.8820/−33.72%. **4 of 7 pre-registered predictions HIT** (P2 FROZEN, P4 DVOL and P7 the selector all missed, and the misses are reported as the finding). **RULES untouched.** A report-only **PROTOCOL clause 12** is proposed for Sunday review: a key that reads the LEVEL of an auto-adjusted price series — raw price rank, frozen-at-entry rank, dollar volume, or any product with a price — is look-ahead by construction and is not a candidate for any book; only ratio keys cancel the factor. Blast radius: 6 LEADERBOARD rows name PRICE/NEG, all from ideas 181/192, and no KEEP-candidate has ever used a price-level key, so no live rule is affected. Ideas 195-197 queued.
